kalyāṇakārakam
atha prathamaḥ paricchedaḥ
maṃgalācaraṇa va āyurvedotpatti
śrīmatsurāsuranareṃdrakirīṭakoṭi--
māṇikyaraśminikarārcitapādapīṭaḥ
tīrthādhipo jitaripurvaṣabho babhūva
sākṣādakāraṇajagattritayaikavaṃdhuḥ || 1
bhāvārthaḥ--The Hindi commentary was not digitized.
bhagavān ādināthase prārthanā
taṃ tīrthanāthamadhigamya vinamya mūrdhnā
satprātihāryavibhavādiparītamūrtim
sapraśrayaṃ trikaraṇīrukṛtapraṇāmāḥ
papracchuritthamakhilaṃ bharateśvarādyāḥ || 2
bhāvārthaḥ--The Hindi commentary was not digitized.
prāgbhoṃgabhūmiṣu janā janitātirāgāḥ
kalpadrumārpitasamastamahopabhogāḥ
divyaṃ sukhaṃ samanubhūya manuṣyabhāve
svargaṃ yayuḥ punarapīṣṭasukhaṃ supuṇyāḥ || 3
bhāvārthaḥ--The Hindi commentary was not digitized.
atropapāda{??}ttamadehivargāḥ
puṇyādhikāstvanapavartyamahāyuṣaste
anye'pavartyaparamāyuṣa eva loke
teṣāṃ mahadbhayamabhūdiha doṣakopāt || 4
bhāvārthaḥ--The Hindi commentary was not digitized.
deva ! tvameva śaraṇaṃ śaraṇāgatānā--
masākimākuladhiyāmiha karmabhūmau
śītāṃtitāpahimavṛṣṭinipīḍitānāṃ
kālakramātkadaśanāśanatatparāṇām || 5
bhāvārthaḥ--The Hindi commentary was not digitized.
nānāvidhāmayabhayādatiduḥkhitānā--
māhārabheṣajaniruktimajānatāṃ naḥ
tatsvāsthyarakṣaṇavidhānamihāturāṇāṃ
kā vā kriyā kathayatāmatha lokanātha ! || 6
bhāvārthaḥ--The Hindi commentary was not digitized.
bhagavānakī divyadhvani
vijñāpya devamiti viśvajagaddhitārthaṃ
tūṣṇīṃ sthitā gaṇadharapramukhāḥ pradhānāḥ
tasminmahāsadasi divyaninādayuktā
vāṇī sasāra sarasā varadevadevī || 7
bhāvārthaḥ--The Hindi commentary was not digitized.
vastucatuṣṭayanirūpaṇa
tatrāditaḥ puruṣalakṣaṇamāmayānā--
mapyauṣadhānyakhilakālaviśeṣaṇaṃ ca
saṃkṣepataḥ sakalavastucatuṣṭayaṃ sā
sarvajñasūcakamidaṃ kathayāṃcakāra || 8
bhāvārthaḥ--The Hindi commentary was not digitized.
āyurvedaśāstrakā paramparāgamanakrama
divyadhvaniprakaṭitaṃ paramārthajātaṃ
sākṣāttathā gaṇadharo'dhijage samastam
paścāt gaṇādhipanirūpitavākprapaṃca--
maṣṭārdhanirmaladhiyo munayo'dhijagmuḥ || 9
bhāvārthaḥ--The Hindi commentary was not digitized.
evaṃ jināṃtaranibaṃdhanasiddhamārgā--
dāyātamāyatamanākulamarthagāḍham
svāyaṃbhuvaṃ sakalameva sanātanaṃ tat--
sākṣācchrutaṃ śrutadalaiḥśrutakevalibhyaḥ || 10
bhāvārthaḥ--The Hindi commentary was not digitized.
graṃthakārakī pratijñā
prodyajjinapravacanāmṛtasāgarāntaḥ--
prodyattaraṃganisṛtālpasuśīkaraṃ vā
vakṣyāmahe sakalalokahitaikadhāma
kalyāṇakārakamiti prathitārthayuktam || 11
bhāvārthaḥ--The Hindi commentary was not digitized.
graṃtharacanākā uddeśa
naivātivākpaṭutayā na ca kāvyadarpā--
nnaivānyaśāstramadabhaṃjanahetunā vā
kiṃtu svakīyatapa ityavadhārya varya--
mācāryamārgamadhigamya vidhāsyate tat || 12
bhāvārthaḥ--The Hindi commentary was not digitized.
svādhyāyamāhurapare tapasāṃ hi mūlaṃ
manye ca vaidyavaravatsalatāpradhānam
tasmāttapaścaraṇameva mayā prayatnā--
dārabhyate svaparasaukhyavidhāyi samyak || 13
bhāvārthaḥ--The Hindi commentary was not digitized.
durjananiṃdā
atrāpi saṃti bahavaḥ kuṭilasvabhāvā
durdṛṣṭayo dvirasanāḥ kumatiprayuktāḥ
chidrābhilāṣaniratāḥ paravādhakāśca
ghororagairupamitāḥ puruṣādhamāste || 14
bhāvārthaḥ--The Hindi commentary was not digitized.
kecitpunaḥ svagṛhamānyaguṇāḥ pareṣāṃ
duṣyaṃtyaśeṣaviduṣāṃ na hi tatra doṣaḥ
pāpātmanāṃ prakṛtireva pareṣvasūyā--
paiśunyavākparuṣalakṣaṇalakṣitānām || 15
bhāvārthaḥ--The Hindi commentary was not digitized.
kecidvicārarahitāḥ prathitapratāpāḥ
sākṣātpiśācasadṛśāḥ pracaraṃti loke
taiḥ kiṃ yathāprakṛtameva mayā prayojyaṃ
mā1tsaryamāryaguṇavaryamiti prasiddham || 16
bhāvārthaḥ--The Hindi commentary was not digitized.
ācāryakā aṃtaraṃga
evaṃ vicārya śithilīkṛtamatsaro'haṃ
śāstraṃ yathādhikṛtamevamudāhariṣye
sarvajñavaktranisṛtaṃ gaṇadevalabdhaṃ
paścāmmahāmuniparaṃparayāvatīrṇam || 17
bhāvārthaḥ--The Hindi commentary was not digitized.
mātsaryamāryagaṇavarjyamiti prasiddhaṃ iti pāṭhāṃtaraṃ
satpuruṣa mātsarvako choḍeṃ aitā lokameṃ prasiddha hai
vaidyaśadvakī vyutpatti
vidyeti satprakaṭakevalalocanākhyā
tasyāṃ yadetadupapannamudāraśāstram
vaidyaṃ vadaṃti padaśāstraviśeṣaṇajñā
etadvicintya ca paṭhaṃti ca te'pi vaidyāḥ || 18
bhāvārthaḥ--The Hindi commentary was not digitized.
āyurvedaśadvakā artha
vedo'yamityapi ca bodhavicāralābhā--
ttatvārthasūcakavacaḥ khalu dhātubhedāt
āyuśca tena saha pūrvanivaddhamudya--
cchāstrābhidhānamaparaṃ pravadaṃti tajjñāḥ || 19
bhāvārthaḥ--The Hindi commentary was not digitized.
śiṣyaguṇalakṣaṇakathanapratijñā
evaṃvidhasya bhuvanaikahitādhikodya--
dvaidyasya bhājanatayā pravikālpitā ye
tānatra sādhuguṇalakṣaṇasāmyarūpā--
nvakṣyāmahe jinapatipratipannamārgāt || 20
bhāvārthaḥ--The Hindi commentary was not digitized.
āyurvedādhyayanayogya śiṣya
rājanyavipravaravaiśyakuleṣu kaści--
ddhīmānaniṃdyacaritaḥ kuśalo vinītaḥ
prātarguruṃ samupasṛtya yathānupṛcchet
so'yaṃ bhavedamalasaṃyamaśāstrabhāgī || 21
bhāvārthaḥ--The Hindi commentary was not digitized.
vaidyavidyādānakrama
jñātasya tasya guṇataḥ suparīkṣitasyā-
pyarhatsamakṣamuparopitasadbratasya
deyaṃ sadā bhavati śāstramidaṃ pradhānaṃ
nānyasya deyamiti vaidyavido vadaṃti || 22
bhāvārthaḥ--The Hindi commentary was not digitized.
vidyāprāptike sādhana
ācāryasādhanasahāyanivāsavalbhā
ārogyabuddhivinayodyamaśāstrarāgāḥ
bāhyāṃtaraṃganijasadguṇasā dhanāni
śāstrārthināṃ satatamevamudāhṛtāni || 23
bhāvārthaḥ--The Hindi commentary was not digitized.
vaidyaśāstrakā pradhānadhyeya
lokopakārakaraṇārthamidaṃ hi śāstraṃ
śāstraprayojanamapi dvividhaṃ yathāvat
svasthasya rakṣaṇamathāmayamokṣaṇaṃ ca
saṃkṣepataḥ sakalameva nirūpyate'tra || 24
bhāvārthaḥ--The Hindi commentary was not digitized.
lokaśadbakā artha
jīvādikān sapadi yatra hi satpadārthān
sasthāvarapravarajaṃgamabhedabhinnān
ālokayaṃti nijasadguṇajātisatvān
lokoyamityabhimato munibhiḥ purāṇaiḥ || 25
bhāvārthaḥ--The Hindi commentary was not digitized.
cikitsāke ādhāra
siddhāṃtataḥ prathitajīvasamāsabhede
paryāptisaṃjñivarapaṃcavidheṃdriyeṣu

kṣetre ca dharmabahule paramārthajātāḥ || 26
bhāvārthaḥ--The Hindi commentary was not digitized.
teṣāṃ kriyā pratidinaṃ kriyate bhiṣagbhi--
rāyurvayo'gnibalasatvasudeśasātmyam
vikhyātasatprakṛtibheṣajadeharogān
kālakramānapi yathakramato viditvā || 27
bhāvārthaḥ--The Hindi commentary was not digitized.
cikitsā ke cāra pāda
tatra kriyeti kathitā munibhiścikitsā
seyaṃ caturvidhapadārthaguṇapradhānā
vaidyāturauṣadhasubhṛtyagaṇāḥ padārthā--
steṣvapyaśeṣadhiṣaṇo bhiṣageva mukhyaḥ || 28
bhāvārthaḥ--The Hindi commentary was not digitized.
vaidyalakṣaṇa
graṃthārthavinmatiyuto'nyamatapravīṇaḥ

dharmādhikaḥ sucarito bahutīrthaśuddho
vaidyo bhavenmatimatāṃ mahatāṃ ca yogyaḥ || 29
bhāvārthaḥ--The Hindi commentary was not digitized.
cikitsāpadhdati
praśnairnimittavidhinā śakunāgamena
jyotirviśeṣataralagnaśaśāṃkayogaiḥ
svapnaiśca divyakathitairapi cāturāṇā--
māyuḥ pramāṇamadhigamya bhiṣagyateta || 30
bhāvārthaḥ--The Hindi commentary was not digitized.
riṣṭairvinā na maraṇaṃ bhavatīha jaṃtoḥ
sthānavyatikramaṇato'tisusūkṣmato vā
kṛcchrāṇyapi prathitabhūtabhavadbhaviṣya--
drūpāṇi yatnavidhinātra bhiṣakprapaśyet || 31
bhāvārthaḥ--The Hindi commentary was not digitized.
ariṣṭalakṣaṇa
riṣṭānyapi prakṛtidehanijasvabhāva--

bhāvārthaḥ--The Hindi commentary was not digitized.
riṣṭa sūcakadūtalakṣaṇa
hīnādhikātikṛśakṛṣṇavirūkṣitāṃgaḥ
savyādhitaḥ svayamathāyudhadaṇḍahastaḥ
saṃdhyāsu sāśrunayano bhayavepamāno
dūto bhavedatitarāṃ yamadūtakalpaḥ || 33
aśvaiḥ kharai rathavaraiḥ karabhaiḥ rathāmyaiḥ
prāptaḥ sadā bhavati dūtagaṇo'tiniṃdyaḥ
yo vā chinatti tṛṇamagragato bhinatti
kāṣṭhāni loṣṭhamathaveṣṭhakamiṣṭakaṃ vā || 34
evaṃvidhaṃ sapadi dūtagataṃ ca riṣṭaṃ
dṛṣṭavāturasya maraṇaikanimittahetum
taṃ varjayediha bhiṣagviditārthasūtraḥ
śubhadūtalakṣaṇa

śubhaśakuna

sāmudrikaśāstranusāra alpāyumahāyuparīkṣā
yasyāti komalatarāvatimāṃsalauca

nānāsurūpayutagāḍhāviśāladīrgha
rekhānvitāvamalināviha pāṇipādau || 41
yasyātipeśalatarāvadhikauca karṇau
nīlotpalābhanayane daśanāstathaiva
muktopamā sassadāḍimabījakalpā
snigdhonnatāyatalalāṭakacauca yasya || 42
yasyāyatāḥ śvasitavīkṣaṇa bāhupuṣṭāḥ
sthūlāstathāṃgulinakhānananāsikāssyuḥ
hrasvā raseṃdriyagalodarameḍhrajaṃghāḥ
nimnāśca saṃdhivaranābhinigūḍhagulphāḥ || 43
yasyātivistṛtamurastanayorbhruvorvā

yasyābhiṣiktamanuliptamihordhvameva
śuṣyeccharīramatha mastakameva paścāt || 44
ājanmanaḥ prabhṛti yasyaḥ hi rogamuktaḥ
kāyaḥ śanaiśca parivṛddhimupaiti nityam
śikṣākalāpamapi yasya matiḥ suśaktā
jñātuṃ ca yasya nikhilāni dṛḍheṃdriyāṇi || 45
susnigdhasūkṣmamṛdukeśacayaśca yasya
prāyastathā praviralāḥ tanuromakūpāḥ
yasyedṛśaṃ vapuraniṃdyasulakṣaṇāṃṅka
tasyādhikaṃ dhanamatīva ca dīrghamāyuḥ || 46
ityevaṃsakalasulakṣaṇaiḥ pumāṃsyā--
ddīrghāyustadaparamardhamāyurardhaiḥ
hīnāyurviditavilakṣaṇasya sākṣā--
ttatsvāsthyaṃ pravaravayo vicāryate'taḥ || 47

siddhānnapānavargaḥ
yavāgū ke guṇa
pacati ca khalu sarvaṃ dīpanī bastiśuddhiṃ

yadyapi isa ślokameṃ āṃvalā, aura baheḍe kī saṃkhyā nideśaṃ ṭhīka taurase nahīṃ kī gaī hai
tathāpi anya aneka vaidyaka graṃthoṃmeṃ prāyaḥ isī prakārakā ullekha milatā hai ki jahāṃpara triphalākā sādhā- raṇa kathana ho vahāṃ uparokta prakārase grahaṇa kiyā jātā hai
isī ādhārase ūpara spaṣṭatayā saṃkhyā ni- deśa kī gaī hai
dūsarī bāta yaha hai ki ślokameṃ baheḍā sevana karanekā samaya nahīṃ batalāyā hai
haraḍake sātha hī khārve to mātrā baḍhatī hai, āṃvale kī mātrā kamatī hotī hai
isa kāraṇase hama yaha samaśate hai ki eka haraḍa, do baheḍā, tīna āṃvalā isa kramase lekara tīnoṃko eka sātha cūrṇa karake yogya mātrāmeṃ śāma subaha sevana karanā cāhiye

kaphanāśaka, auṣadhiyoṃ ke samuccaya
yattiktaṃ yacca rūkṣaṃ yadapi ca kaṭukaṃ yatkaṣāṃyaṃ viśuṣkaṃ
yatkṣāraṃ yacca tīkṣṇaṃ yadapi ca viśadaṃ yallaghudravyamuṣṇaṃ

tuga nahīṃ hai
tugaśabda se anya kisī auṣadhakā bodha nahīṃ hotā hai
tathā tālīsādi cūrṇameṃ vaṃśalocana ātā hai
vaha kapha nāśaka bhī haiṃ
isaliye isa ko grahaṇa kara sakate haiṃ
